Aetherheart – Piece 15
(Config card parsing + state sync from cards + ON/OFF enforcement)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 15: Read Configure card, sync toggles,
// enforce system presence
// ============================================================

function parseOnOff(block, key) {
  if (!block) return null;
  const re = new RegExp(key + "\\s*:\\s*(ON|OFF)", "i");
  const m = block.match(re);
  if (!m) return null;
  return m[1].toUpperCase() === "ON";
}

function parseValue(block, key) {
  if (!block) return null;
  const re = new RegExp(key + "\\s*:\\s*([^\\n]+)", "i");
  const m = block.match(re);
  return m ? m[1].trim() : null;
}

function readConfigureCard(A) {
  const card = findCard("Configure Aetherheart");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;

  const e = card.entry;

  // Preset + Rating
  const preset = parseValue(e, "Preset");
  if (preset) {
    const p = preset.split(/\s|\|/)[0].toLowerCase();
    if (p) A.settings.preset = p;
  }
  const rating = parseValue(e, "Rating");
  if (rating) A.settings.rating = rating.split(/\s|\|/)[0];

  // Core toggles
  const map = [
    ["Script", "scriptEnabled"],
    ["Inventory", "inventoryEnabled"],
    ["Currency", "currencyEnabled"],
    ["Time", "timeEnabled"],
    ["Bills", "billsEnabled"],
    ["Currency context", "currencyContext"],
    ["Narration System", "narrationSystem"],
    ["Imperial units", "imperial"],
    ["RP System", "rpSystem"],
    ["Inner Thoughts", "innerThoughts"],
    ["Inner Opinions", "innerOpinions"],
    ["Card Creator", "cardCreator"],
    ["Scheduling", "scheduling"],
    ["Bounty Board", "bountyBoard"],
    ["Job Board", "jobBoard"],
    ["Quests", "quests"]
  ];
  map.forEach(([label, key]) => {
    const v = parseOnOff(e, label);
    if (v !== null) A.settings[key] = v;
  });
}

function readRPSystemCard(A) {
  const card = findCard("RP System");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  const e = card.entry;
  const map = [
    ["Vitals", "vitals"],
    ["Survival", "survival"],
    ["Leveling", "leveling"],
    ["Skills", "skills"],
    ["Abilities", "abilities"],
    ["Powers", "powers"],
    ["Loot", "loot"],
    ["Ammo", "ammo"],
    ["d20", "d20"],
    ["Fuel", "fuel"],
    ["Reputation", "reputation"],
    ["Jail", "jail"],
    ["Momentum", "momentum"],
    ["Deus Ex", "deusEx"],
    ["Anti-Deus Ex", "antiDeusEx"]
  ];
  map.forEach(([label, key]) => {
    const v = parseOnOff(e, label);
    if (v !== null) A.settings[key] = v;
  });
  const fuelName = parseValue(e, "Fuel Name");
  if (fuelName) A.settings.fuelName = fuelName;
  const magic = parseValue(e, "Magic Mode");
  if (magic) {
    const m = magic.toLowerCase();
    if (m.indexOf("wizard") === 0) A.settings.magicMode = "wizarding";
    else if (m.indexOf("sorc") === 0) A.settings.magicMode = "sorcery";
  }
  // Leveling off kills dependents
  if (!A.settings.leveling) {
    A.settings.skills = false;
    A.settings.abilities = false;
    A.settings.powers = false;
    A.settings.loot = false;
  }
}

function readTimeCard(A) {
  const card = findCard("Configure Time");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  const e = card.entry;
  const tpt = parseValue(e, "Time/Turn");
  if (tpt) {
    A.time = A.time || {};
    A.time.timePerTurn = tpt.split(/\s/)[0];
  }
  const imperial = parseOnOff(e, "Imperial units");
  if (imperial !== null) A.settings.imperial = imperial;
}

function readVitalsNumbers(A) {
  const card = findCard("Vitals");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  const e = card.entry;
  function num(label) {
    const m = e.match(new RegExp(label + "\\s*:\\s*(\\d+)", "i"));
    return m ? parseInt(m[1], 10) : null;
  }
  const hp = num("HP");
  const mp = num("MP");
  const sta = num("Stamina");
  const hunger = num("Hunger");
  const thirst = num("Thirst");
  const tired = num("Tiredness");
  if (hp !== null) A.vitals.hp = hp;
  if (mp !== null) A.vitals.mp = mp;
  if (sta !== null) A.vitals.stamina = sta;
  if (hunger !== null) A.survival.hunger = hunger;
  if (thirst !== null) A.survival.thirst = thirst;
  if (tired !== null) A.survival.tiredness = tired;
}

function syncFromCards(A) {
  readConfigureCard(A);
  readRPSystemCard(A);
  readTimeCard(A);
  readVitalsNumbers(A);
}

// Library and Output now sync first

  const A = ensureState();
  syncFromCards(A);
  updateAllCards(A);
  updatePiece11Cards(A);
  updatePiece12Cards(A);
  updatePiece13Cards(A);
  updatePiece14Cards(A);
}

  if (sched) {
    if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
    state.memory.frontMemory = (state.memory.frontMemory || "") + "\n" + sched;

  updateAllCards(A);
  updatePiece11Cards(A);
  updatePiece12Cards(A);
  updatePiece13Cards(A);
  updatePiece14Cards(A);

  if (checkGameOver(A)) {
    text = buildGameOverScreen();
  }

  return text;
}

  return text;

Aetherheart – Piece 16
(ADD command parsers + Inventory/Quest/Bounty card reading)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 16: ADD parsers, card list readers
// ============================================================

function handleAddCommands(text, A) {
  const lines = text.split(/\n/);
  let msgs = [];
  lines.forEach(line => {
    let m;

    // Skills / Abilities / Powers
    m = line.match(/\[ADD SKILL:\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
    if (m) {
      A.skills = A.skills || [];
      A.skills.push(m[1].trim());
      msgs.push("Skill added: " + m[1].trim());
    }
    m = line.match(/\[ADD ABILITY:\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
    if (m) {
      A.abilities = A.abilities || [];
      A.abilities.push(m[1].trim());
      msgs.push("Ability added: " + m[1].trim());
    }
    m = line.match(/\[ADD POWER:\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
    if (m) {
      A.powers = A.powers || [];
      A.powers.push(m[1].trim());
      msgs.push("Power added: " + m[1].trim());
    }

    // Bounty
    m = line.match(/\[ADD BOUNTY:\s*([^\]|]+)\|\s*([^\]|]+)\|\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
    if (m) {
      A.bounties = A.bounties || [];
      A.bounties.push({
        name: m[1].trim(),
        condition: m[2].trim(),
        reward: m[3].trim()
      });
      msgs.push("Bounty posted: " + m[1].trim());
    }

    // Job
    m = line.match(/\[ADD JOB:\s*([^\]|]+)\|\s*([^\]|]+)\|\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
    if (m) {
      A.jobs = A.jobs || [];
      A.jobs.push({
        title: m[1].trim(),
        pay: m[2].trim(),
        desc: m[3].trim()
      });
      msgs.push("Job posted: " + m[1].trim());
    }

    // Quest
    m = line.match(/\[ADD QUEST:\s*([^\]|]+)(?:\|\s*([^\]]+))?\]/i);
    if (m) {
      A.quests = A.quests || [];
      if (A.quests.length < 5) {
        A.quests.push({ title: m[1].trim(), progress: 0, desc: (m[2] || "").trim() });
        msgs.push("Quest added: " + m[1].trim());
      }
    }

    // Route
    m = line.match(/\[ADD ROUTE:\s*([^\]|]+)\|\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
    if (m) {
      A.routes = A.routes || {};
      A.routes[m[1].trim()] = m[2].trim();
      msgs.push("Route added: " + m[1].trim());
    }

    // Schedule block
    m = line.match(/\[ADD:\s*([^\]|]+)\|\s*([^\]|]+)\|\s*([^\]|]+)\|\s*([^\]|]+)\|\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
    if (m) {
      A.schedule = A.schedule || {};
      const key = m[2].trim();
      if (!A.schedule[key]) A.schedule[key] = [];
      A.schedule[key].push({
        name: m[1].trim(),
        start: m[3].trim(),
        end: m[4].trim(),
        desc: m[5].trim(),
        on: true
      });
      msgs.push("Schedule block added: " + m[1].trim());
    }
  });
  return msgs;
}

function readCharacterSheetAdds(A) {
  const card = findCard("Character Sheet");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  // Allow ADD lines written directly on the sheet
  const msgs = handleAddCommands(card.entry, A);
  return msgs;
}

function readBountyBoardCard(A) {
  const card = findCard("Bounty Board");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  handleAddCommands(card.entry, A);
}

function readJobBoardCard(A) {
  const card = findCard("Job Board");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  handleAddCommands(card.entry, A);
}

function readScheduleCard(A) {
  const card = findCard("Schedule Time Blocks");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  handleAddCommands(card.entry, A);
}

function syncListsFromCards(A) {
  readCharacterSheetAdds(A);
  readBountyBoardCard(A);
  readJobBoardCard(A);
  readScheduleCard(A);
}

// Extend input to catch ADD lines typed in story action
function onInput_Aetherheart(text) {
  const A = ensureState();
  syncFromCards(A);
  if (!A.settings.scriptEnabled) return text;

  if (handleSaveLoadCommands(text, A)) return text;

  const addMsgs = handleAddCommands(text, A);
  if (addMsgs.length && !A.settings.silentMode) {
    state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
      addMsgs.map(m => "[SYSTEM] " + m).join("\n");
  }

  const d20Result = handleD20(text, A);
  if (d20Result) state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") + d20Result;

  processTurnSystems(text, A);
  maybeLootLetter(A, text);
  compressThoughts(A);
  syncListsFromCards(A);

  const cmd = text.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (cmd === "/status") state.message = buildStatusCard(A);
  if (cmd === "/help") state.message = buildHelpCard();
  if (cmd === "/keywords") state.message = buildKeywordsCard();
  if (cmd === "/jailbreak") {
    A.settings.jailbreak = true;
    state.message = "Jailbreak active. Story and persona suspended.";
  }

  return text;
}

Worked for 2s
Aetherheart – Piece 17
(Card Creator /card + title markers + memory note updates)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 17: /card command, title markers,
// Inner Thoughts note updates
// ============================================================

function detectCardType(details) {
  const t = (details || "").toLowerCase();
  if (/location|town|city|shop|tavern|room|building|street/.test(t)) return "Location";
  if (/person|man|woman|character|npc|ally|enemy|girl|boy/.test(t)) return "Character";
  return "Other";
}

function buildGeneratedEntry(type, title, details, A) {
  let entry = "";
  if (A.settings.innerThoughts) {
    entry += "{title: " + title + "}\n";
  }
  if (type === "Character") {
    entry += "Name: " + title + "\n";
    entry += "Appearance: concise physical description pending\n";
    entry += "Personality: \n";
    entry += "Background: \n";
    if (details) entry += "Notes: " + details + "\n";
  } else if (type === "Location") {
    entry += "Name: " + title + "\n";
    entry += "Description: " + (details || "") + "\n";
  } else {
    entry += "Name: " + title + "\n";
    entry += "Description: " + (details || "") + "\n";
  }
  return entry;
}

function handleCardCommand(text, A) {
  // /card (type) (title) (details)
  const m = text.match(/^\/card\s+(character|location|other)?\s*([^\n]+)$/i);
  if (!m) return false;

  let type = (m[1] || "").trim();
  let rest = m[2].trim();
  // title is first chunk, details after
  let title = rest;
  let details = "";
  const sp = rest.indexOf(" ");
  if (sp > 0) {
    // allow: /card Character Bob tall scarred man
    const parts = rest.split(/\s+/);
    if (!type) {
      // try first word as type
      const maybe = parts[0].toLowerCase();
      if (maybe === "character" || maybe === "location" || maybe === "other") {
        type = maybe;
        parts.shift();
      }
    }
    title = parts[0];
    details = parts.slice(1).join(" ");
  }
  if (!type) type = detectCardType(details || title);
  type = type.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + type.slice(1).toLowerCase();

  const entry = buildGeneratedEntry(type, title, details, A);
  const category = "· Aetherheart (Card Creator)";
  writeCard(title, entry, category);

  if (!A.settings.silentMode) {
    state.message = "[SYSTEM] Card created: " + title + " (" + type + ")";
  }
  // Suspend normal story continuation feel
  return true;
}

function updateMemoryNotes(A, name, sentence) {
  if (!A.settings.innerThoughts) return;
  A.thoughts = A.thoughts || {};
  if (!A.thoughts[name]) A.thoughts[name] = "";
  // append short declarative memory
  A.thoughts[name] = (A.thoughts[name] + " " + sentence).trim();
  // keep relatively short
  if (A.thoughts[name].length > 400) {
    A.thoughts[name] = A.thoughts[name].slice(-350);
  }
  // write into the card notes if the card exists
  const card = findCard(name);
  if (card) {
    let entry = card.entry || "";
    if (entry.indexOf("{title:") === -1 && A.settings.innerThoughts) {
      entry = "{title: " + name + "}\n" + entry;
    }
    // replace or append Notes line
    if (/Notes:/i.test(entry)) {
      entry = entry.replace(/Notes:.*$/im, "Notes: " + A.thoughts[name]);
    } else {
      entry = entry + "\nNotes: " + A.thoughts[name];
    }
    card.entry = entry;
  }
}

function autoTitleExistingCards(A) {
  if (!A.settings.innerThoughts) return;
  if (!Array.isArray(storyCards)) return;
  storyCards.forEach(c => {
    if (!c || !c.title) return;
    if (c.title.indexOf("Configure") === 0) return;
    if (c.title.indexOf("_") === 0) return;
    if (!c.entry) c.entry = "";
    if (c.entry.indexOf("{title:") === -1) {
      c.entry = "{title: " + c.title + "}\n" + c.entry;
    }
  });
}

function onInput_Aetherheart(text) {
  const A = ensureState();
  syncFromCards(A);
  if (!A.settings.scriptEnabled) return text;

  if (handleSaveLoadCommands(text, A)) return text;
  if (handleCardCommand(text, A)) return text;

  const addMsgs = handleAddCommands(text, A);
  if (addMsgs.length && !A.settings.silentMode) {
    state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
      addMsgs.map(m => "[SYSTEM] " + m).join("\n");
  }

  const d20Result = handleD20(text, A);
  if (d20Result) state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") + d20Result;

  processTurnSystems(text, A);
  maybeLootLetter(A, text);
  compressThoughts(A);
  syncListsFromCards(A);
  autoTitleExistingCards(A);

  const cmd = text.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (cmd === "/status") state.message = buildStatusCard(A);
  if (cmd === "/help") state.message = buildHelpCard();
  if (cmd === "/keywords") state.message = buildKeywordsCard();
  if (cmd === "/jailbreak") {
    A.settings.jailbreak = true;
    state.message = "Jailbreak active. Story and persona suspended.";
  }

  return text;
}

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 18: Front Memory status, Weather,
// Bills/Income, context injection
// ============================================================

function buildFrontStatus(A) {
  const parts = [];
  if (A.settings.timeEnabled && A.time) {
    parts.push(getMonthName(A.time.month) + " " + A.time.day + ", Year " + A.time.year + " " + formatHour(A.time.hour));
  }
  if (A.settings.vitals && A.vitals) {
    const v = A.vitals;
    parts.push("HP " + v.hp + "/" + v.maxHp + " MP " + v.mp + "/" + v.maxMp + " STA " + v.stamina + "/" + v.maxStamina);
  }
  if (A.settings.survival && A.survival) {
    const s = A.survival;
    parts.push("Hunger " + s.hunger + "% Thirst " + s.thirst + "% Tired " + s.tiredness + "%");
  }
  if (A.settings.fuel) {
    parts.push((A.settings.fuelName || "Fuel") + " " + (A.fuel || 100) + "%");
  }
  if (A.settings.leveling) {
    parts.push("Lv " + A.level);
  }
  if (A.settings.momentum) {
    parts.push(buildMomentumLine(A));
  }
  if (!parts.length) return "";
  return "[Status: " + parts.join(" | ") + "]";
}

function injectFrontMemory(A) {
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  const status = buildFrontStatus(A);
  const sched = A.settings.scheduling ? enforceSchedule(A, "") : "";
  let fm = "";
  if (status) fm += status + "\n";
  if (sched) fm += sched + "\n";
  if (A.settings.deusEx) fm += "[Deus Ex active]\n";
  if (A.settings.antiDeusEx) fm += "[Anti-Deus Ex active]\n";
  if (fm) {
    state.memory.frontMemory = (state.memory.frontMemory || "") + fm;
  }
}

Seasonal mood follows preset and calendar.

[Realmheart-compatible weather injection style]`;

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 19: Currency, Ledger, Purchases
// ============================================================

function buildLedgerCard(A) {
  const ledger = A.ledger || [];
  let lines = ["== LEDGER ==", "Balance: " + (A.balance || 0), ""];
  if (!ledger.length) {
    lines.push("(no transactions)");
  } else {
    ledger.slice(-12).forEach(e => lines.push(e));
  }
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("[Auto-updated on purchases, bounties, jobs, bills]");
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function ledgerAdd(A, note, amount) {
  A.ledger = A.ledger || [];
  const sign = amount >= 0 ? "+" : "";
  A.ledger.push(sign + amount + "  " + note);
  if (A.ledger.length > 40) A.ledger = A.ledger.slice(-30);
  A.balance = (A.balance || 0) + amount;
}

function handleCurrencySpeech(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.currencyEnabled) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];

  // simple pay / buy patterns
  let m = t.match(/\b(?:pay|spend|tip)\s+(\d+)/);
  if (m) {
    const amt = parseInt(m[1], 10);
    if ((A.balance || 0) >= amt) {
      ledgerAdd(A, "Payment", -amt);
      msgs.push("Paid " + amt);
    } else {
      msgs.push("Insufficient funds");
    }
  }

  m = t.match(/\b(?:buy|purchase)\s+(?:a |an |the )?([a-z0-9\s\-']{2,40})(?:\s+for\s+(\d+))?/);
  if (m) {
    const item = m[1].trim();
    const price = m[2] ? parseInt(m[2], 10) : 10;
    if ((A.balance || 0) >= price) {
      ledgerAdd(A, "Bought " + item, -price);
      inventoryAdd(A, "other", item, 1);
      msgs.push("Bought " + item + " for " + price);
    } else {
      msgs.push("Cannot afford " + item);
    }
  }

  m = t.match(/\b(?:receive|earn|get paid|collect)\s+(\d+)/);
  if (m) {
    const amt = parseInt(m[1], 10);
    ledgerAdd(A, "Income", amt);
    msgs.push("Received " + amt);
  }

  if (/\bcheck (my )?(balance|wallet|money)\b/.test(t)) {
    msgs.push("Balance: " + (A.balance || 0));
  }

  return msgs;
}

function updatePiece19Cards(A) {
  if (A.settings.currencyEnabled) {
    writeCard("Ledger", buildLedgerCard(A), "· Aetherheart");
  }
}

// Hook currency into turn processing
function processTurnSystems(text, A) {
  const minutes = parseTimePerTurn(A.time && A.time.timePerTurn);
  advanceTime(A, minutes);
  tickSurvival(A, minutes);

  let msgs = [];
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleInventoryVerbs(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleBountyActions(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleJobActions(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleRestAndFuel(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleCurrencySpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(expandNaturalLanguage(text, A));

  if (msgs.length && !A.settings.silentMode) {
    state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
      msgs.map(m => "[SYSTEM] " + m).join("\n");
  }
}

function onOutput_Aetherheart(text) {
  const A = ensureState();
  if (!A.settings.scriptEnabled) return text;

  syncFromCards(A);
  text = filterBannedPhrases(text);
  applyStandardRules(A);
  processBills(A);
  injectFrontMemory(A);

  updateAllCards(A);
  updatePiece11Cards(A);
  updatePiece12Cards(A);
  updatePiece13Cards(A);
  updatePiece14Cards(A);
  updatePiece18Cards(A);
  updatePiece19Cards(A);

  if (checkGameOver(A)) text = buildGameOverScreen();
  return text;
}

function memoryOnlyReset(A) {
  A.thoughts = {};
  A.opinions = {};
  A.charMemory = {};
  A.journal = [];
  A.lore = [];
}

function fullReset(A) {
  const preset = A.settings.preset;
  const rating = A.settings.rating;
  state.aether = null;
  ensureState();
  state.aether.settings.preset = preset;
  state.aether.settings.rating = rating;
}

function handleResetCommands(text, A) {
  const cmd = text.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (cmd === "/reset memory") {
    memoryOnlyReset(A);
    state.message = "[SYSTEM] Memory-only reset done.";
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd === "/reset all") {
    fullReset(A);
    state.message = "[SYSTEM] Full reset done. Preset and rating kept.";
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

Aetherheart – Piece 21

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 21: XP/Level-up, Drugs, Location hours
// ============================================================

function gainXP(A, amount) {
  if (!A.settings.leveling) return;
  A.xp = (A.xp || 0) + (amount || 0);
  while (A.xp >= (A.xpToNext || 1000)) {
    A.xp -= A.xpToNext;
    A.level = (A.level || 1) + 1;
    A.xpToNext = Math.floor((A.xpToNext || 1000) * 1.35);
    // small vitals bump
    if (A.vitals) {
      A.vitals.maxHp += 5;
      A.vitals.hp = A.vitals.maxHp;
      A.vitals.maxStamina += 3;
      A.vitals.stamina = A.vitals.maxStamina;
    }
    if (!A.settings.silentMode) {
      state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
        "[SYSTEM] Level up! Now level " + A.level;
    }
    // level loot flavor name available via generateLevelLoot
  }
}

function handleXPSpeech(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.leveling) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  if (/\b(defeat|killed|slay|slain|won the fight|victory)\b/.test(t)) {
    gainXP(A, 50);
    msgs.push("XP gained");
  }
  if (/\b(complete(d)? the quest|quest complete)\b/.test(t)) {
    gainXP(A, 100);
    msgs.push("Quest XP gained");
  }
  return msgs;
}

function handleDrugUse(text, A) {
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  // mirrors Configure card defaults; expand with parsed drug list later
  if (/\b(smoke|hit|use)\b.*\b(blunt|joint|weed)\b/.test(t) ||
      /\b(blunt|joint|weed)\b.*\b(smoke|hit|use)\b/.test(t)) {
    if (A.settings.survival) {
      A.survival.hunger = Math.min(100, (A.survival.hunger || 0) + 20);
      A.survival.thirst = Math.min(100, (A.survival.thirst || 0) + 40);
      A.survival.tiredness = Math.min(100, (A.survival.tiredness || 0) + 30);
    }
    if (A.settings.vitals && A.vitals) {
      A.vitals.stamina = Math.max(0, A.vitals.stamina - 10);
    }
    msgs.push("Used weed");
  }
  return msgs;
}

function parseLocationHours(entry) {
  // {hours: 6:00 AM | 5:00 PM} or hours: ...
  if (!entry) return null;
  let m = entry.match(/\{hours:\s*([^|]+)\|\s*([^}]+)\}/i);
  if (!m) m = entry.match(/hours:\s*([^|]+)\|\s*([^\n]+)/i);
  if (!m) return null;
  return { open: m[1].trim(), close: m[2].trim() };
}

function parseCharacterShift(entry) {
  // {shift: Tavern | 8:00 PM | 2:00AM}
  if (!entry) return null;
  const m = entry.match(/\{shift:\s*([^|]+)\|\s*([^|]+)\|\s*([^}]+)\}/i);
  if (!m) return null;
  return { place: m[1].trim(), start: m[2].trim(), end: m[3].trim() };
}

function scanLocationAndShiftCards(A) {
  if (!Array.isArray(storyCards)) return;
  A.locationHours = A.locationHours || {};
  A.characterShifts = A.characterShifts || {};
  storyCards.forEach(c => {
    if (!c || !c.entry) return;
    const hours = parseLocationHours(c.entry);
    if (hours) A.locationHours[c.title] = hours;
    const shift = parseCharacterShift(c.entry);
    if (shift) A.characterShifts[c.title] = shift;
  });
}

function buildLocationShiftInfoCard(A) {
  let lines = ["== HOURS & SHIFTS ==", ""];
  const lh = A.locationHours || {};
  const cs = A.characterShifts || {};
  const locKeys = Object.keys(lh);
  const shiftKeys = Object.keys(cs);
  if (!locKeys.length && !shiftKeys.length) {
    lines.push("(none detected)");
  } else {
    locKeys.forEach(k => lines.push(k + " hours: " + lh[k].open + " - " + lh[k].close));
    shiftKeys.forEach(k => lines.push(k + " shift: " + cs[k].place + " " + cs[k].start + "-" + cs[k].end));
  }
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("[Put {hours: open | close} in location cards]");
  lines.push("[Put {shift: Place | start | end} in character cards]");
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function updatePiece21Cards(A) {
  if (A.settings.scheduling || A.settings.timeEnabled) {
    writeCard("Hours & Shifts", buildLocationShiftInfoCard(A), "· Aetherheart (Scheduling)");
  }
}

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 22: Were/Mer triggers, Schedule day cards
// ============================================================

function ensureTransformConfig(A) {
  if (!A.transform) {
    A.transform = {
      daylight: true,
      everyNight: true,
      anger: false,
      timeBased: true,
      feedingRestores: "HP",
      permWhile: "",
      permAfter: "",
      form: "human" // human | beast | mer
    };
  }
  return A.transform;
}

function readWereMerCard(A) {
  const card = findCard("Were / Mer");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  const e = card.entry;
  const t = ensureTransformConfig(A);
  const dl = parseOnOff(e, "Can go out in daylight");
  if (dl !== null) t.daylight = dl;
  const en = parseOnOff(e, "Transforms every night");
  if (en !== null) t.everyNight = en;
  const anger = parseOnOff(e, "Anger trigger");
  if (anger !== null) t.anger = anger;
  const tb = parseOnOff(e, "Time-based trigger");
  if (tb !== null) t.timeBased = tb;
}

function isNight(A) {
  const h = (A.time && A.time.hour) || 0;
  return h >= 20 || h < 6;
}

function handleTransformation(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.rpSystem) return [];
  const t = ensureTransformConfig(A);
  const msgs = [];
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();

  // anger trigger
  if (t.anger && /\b(rage|fury|enraged|lose control|berserk)\b/.test(lower)) {
    t.form = "beast";
    msgs.push("Transformation triggered by anger");
  }

  // time-based / every night
  if (t.timeBased && t.everyNight && isNight(A) && t.form === "human") {
    t.form = "beast";
    msgs.push("Night transformation");
  }
  if (t.timeBased && !isNight(A) && t.form === "beast" && t.daylight === false) {
    // stays beast if cannot be in daylight as human — simplified
  }
  if (!isNight(A) && t.form === "beast" && t.daylight) {
    // optional auto-revert at dawn
    // t.form = "human";
  }

  // feeding
  if (/\b(feed|feeding|drink blood|hunt)\b/.test(lower) && t.form !== "human") {
    if (t.feedingRestores === "HP" && A.vitals) {
      A.vitals.hp = Math.min(A.vitals.maxHp, A.vitals.hp + 20);
      msgs.push("Feeding restored HP");
    }
  }

  // mer flavor
  if (/\b(enter the water|dive|swim out)\b/.test(lower) && /\bmer\b|mermaid|merman/.test(lower)) {
    t.form = "mer";
    msgs.push("Mer form");
  }

  return msgs;
}

function ensureScheduleDayCard(A, dayKey) {
  const blocks = (A.schedule && A.schedule[dayKey]) || [];
  let lines = ["== " + dayKey.toUpperCase() + " ==", ""];
  if (!blocks.length) {
    lines.push("(no blocks)");
  } else {
    blocks.forEach(b => {
      const flag = b.on === false ? "[OFF]" : "[ON]";
      lines.push(flag + " " + b.name + " | " + b.start + " | " + b.end + " | " + b.desc);
    });
  }
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("[Replace [ON] with [DEL] to remove a block]");
  writeCard(dayKey, lines.join("\n"), "· Aetherheart (Scheduling)");
}

function syncScheduleDayCards(A) {
  if (!A.settings.scheduling) return;
  const sched = A.schedule || {};
  Object.keys(sched).forEach(dayKey => ensureScheduleDayCard(A, dayKey));
}

function handleScheduleDel(text, A) {
  const msgs = [];
  let m = text.match(/\[DEL:\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
  if (!m) return msgs;
  const target = m[1].trim();
  // delete whole day
  if (A.schedule && A.schedule[target]) {
    delete A.schedule[target];
    msgs.push("Removed schedule day: " + target);
    return msgs;
  }
  // delete block by name
  const sched = A.schedule || {};
  Object.keys(sched).forEach(day => {
    const before = sched[day].length;
    sched[day] = sched[day].filter(b => b.name.toLowerCase() !== target.toLowerCase());
    if (sched[day].length !== before) msgs.push("Removed block: " + target);
  });
  return msgs;
}

// Extend processTurnSystems
function processTurnSystems(text, A) {
  const minutes = parseTimePerTurn(A.time && A.time.timePerTurn);
  advanceTime(A, minutes);
  tickSurvival(A, minutes);
  readWereMerCard(A);

  let msgs = [];
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleInventoryVerbs(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleBountyActions(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleJobActions(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleRestAndFuel(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleCurrencySpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleXPSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleDrugUse(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleTransformation(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleScheduleDel(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(expandNaturalLanguage(text, A));

  if (msgs.length && !A.settings.silentMode) {
    state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
      msgs.map(m => "[SYSTEM] " + m).join("\n");
  }
}

function onOutput_Aetherheart(text) {
  const A = ensureState();
  if (!A.settings.scriptEnabled) return text;

  syncFromCards(A);
  text = filterBannedPhrases(text);
  applyStandardRules(A);
  processBills(A);
  injectFrontMemory(A);
  scanLocationAndShiftCards(A);
  syncScheduleDayCards(A);

  updateAllCards(A);
  updatePiece11Cards(A);
  updatePiece12Cards(A);
  updatePiece13Cards(A);
  updatePiece14Cards(A);
  updatePiece18Cards(A);
  updatePiece19Cards(A);
  buildEventsAndArc(A);
  updatePiece21Cards(A);

  if (checkGameOver(A)) text = buildGameOverScreen();
  return text;
}

Aetherheart – Piece 23
(Injury fade + Scars + Heat decay + Jail time)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 23: Injuries, Scars, Heat decay, Jail
// ============================================================

function ensureInjuries(A) {
  if (!A.injuries) A.injuries = [];
  if (!A.scars) A.scars = [];
  return A;
}

function addInjury(A, name, severity) {
  ensureInjuries(A);
  A.injuries.push({ name: name, severity: severity || 1, turns: 0 });
}

function tickInjuries(A) {
  if (!A.settings.vitals) return;
  ensureInjuries(A);
  const keep = [];
  A.injuries.forEach(inj => {
    inj.turns = (inj.turns || 0) + 1;
    // fade: low severity clears faster
    const fadeAfter = 8 - Math.min(5, inj.severity || 1);
    if (inj.turns >= fadeAfter) {
      // chance to leave a scar
      if ((inj.severity || 1) >= 3) {
        A.scars.push(inj.name);
      }
    } else {
      keep.push(inj);
    }
  });
  A.injuries = keep;
}

function buildInjuriesCard(A) {
  ensureInjuries(A);
  let lines = ["== INJURIES & SCARS ==", ""];
  if (!A.injuries.length) lines.push("Injuries: (none)");
  else {
    lines.push("Injuries:");
    A.injuries.forEach(i => lines.push("- " + i.name + " (sev " + i.severity + ")"));
  }
  lines.push("");
  if (!A.scars.length) lines.push("Scars: (none)");
  else {
    lines.push("Scars:");
    A.scars.forEach(s => lines.push("- " + s));
  }
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("[Injuries fade over turns. Severe ones can leave scars.]");
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function tickHeat(A) {
  if (!A.settings.reputation) return;
  A.reputation = A.reputation || { law: 0, social: 0, bounty: 0, heat: 0 };
  if (A.reputation.heat > 0) {
    // slow natural decay
    A.reputation.heat = Math.max(0, A.reputation.heat - 1);
  }
}

function startJail(A, hours) {
  if (!A.settings.jail || !A.settings.timeEnabled) return;
  A.jail = { remaining: hours || 24, active: true };
  if (!A.settings.silentMode) {
    state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
      "[SYSTEM] Jailed for " + (hours || 24) + " hours.";
  }
}

function tickJail(A, minutes) {
  if (!A.jail || !A.jail.active) return;
  A.jail.remaining = Math.max(0, (A.jail.remaining || 0) - ((minutes || 5) / 60));
  if (A.jail.remaining <= 0) {
    A.jail.active = false;
    if (!A.settings.silentMode) {
      state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
        "[SYSTEM] Released from jail.";
    }
  }
}

function handleInjurySpeech(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.vitals) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  if (/\b(wound|injured|bleeding|broken arm|broken leg|cut|stabbed|shot)\b/.test(t)) {
    let name = "Wound";
    if (/broken arm/.test(t)) name = "Broken arm";
    else if (/broken leg/.test(t)) name = "Broken leg";
    else if (/shot/.test(t)) name = "Gunshot";
    else if (/stabbed/.test(t)) name = "Stab wound";
    addInjury(A, name, /broken|shot|stabbed/.test(t) ? 3 : 1);
    msgs.push("Injury recorded: " + name);
  }
  return msgs;
}

function handleJailSpeech(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.jail) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  if (/\b(arrested|thrown in jail|locked up|sent to jail)\b/.test(t)) {
    startJail(A, 24);
    msgs.push("Jail started");
  }
  return msgs;
}

function updatePiece23Cards(A) {
  if (A.settings.vitals) {
    writeCard("Injuries & Scars", buildInjuriesCard(A), "· Aetherheart (RP System)");
  }
}

Aetherheart – Piece 24
(Consequence Memory + Scene Anchor + Rumor Mill + Legacy)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 24: Consequence Memory, Scene Anchor,
// Rumor Mill, Legacy actions
// ============================================================

function ensureStoryExtras(A) {
  if (!A.consequences) A.consequences = [];
  if (!A.rumors) A.rumors = [];
  if (!A.legacy) A.legacy = [];
  if (!A.sceneAnchor) A.sceneAnchor = { location: "", characters: [] };
  return A;
}

function addConsequence(A, line) {
  ensureStoryExtras(A);
  A.consequences.push(line);
  if (A.consequences.length > 12) A.consequences = A.consequences.slice(-10);
}

function addLegacy(A, line) {
  ensureStoryExtras(A);
  A.legacy.push(line);
  if (A.legacy.length > 20) A.legacy = A.legacy.slice(-15);
}

function addRumor(A, line) {
  ensureStoryExtras(A);
  A.rumors.push(line);
  if (A.rumors.length > 10) A.rumors = A.rumors.slice(-8);
}

function buildConsequenceCard(A) {
  ensureStoryExtras(A);
  let lines = ["== CONSEQUENCE MEMORY ==", "Major cause-and-effect log", ""];
  if (!A.consequences.length) lines.push("(none)");
  else A.consequences.forEach(c => lines.push("• " + c));
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("[Short lines only. AI is told to respect these.]");
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function buildRumorCard(A) {
  ensureStoryExtras(A);
  let lines = ["== RUMOR MILL ==", "Local talk that rotates with time and actions", ""];
  if (!A.rumors.length) lines.push("(none yet)");
  else A.rumors.forEach(r => lines.push("• " + r));
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("[Updates from play. Keep entries short.]");
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function buildLegacyCard(A) {
  ensureStoryExtras(A);
  let lines = ["== LEGACY ==", "Permanent one-line records of major deeds", ""];
  if (!A.legacy.length) lines.push("(none)");
  else A.legacy.forEach(l => lines.push("• " + l));
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("[These can be referenced years later in-world.]");
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function buildSceneAnchorCard(A) {
  ensureStoryExtras(A);
  const a = A.sceneAnchor;
  return `== SCENE ANCHOR ==
Location: ${a.location || "(unset)"}
Active characters: ${(a.characters || []).join(", ") || "(unset)"}

[Keeps the AI from teleporting people or forgetting where everyone is]
[Edit directly if needed]`;
}

function handleConsequenceSpeech(text, A) {
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  if (/\b(i (killed|murdered|spared|saved|betrayed|freed|burned|destroyed))\b/.test(t)) {
    const m = t.match(/\bi (killed|murdered|spared|saved|betrayed|freed|burned|destroyed) ([a-z0-9\s\-']{2,40})/);
    if (m) {
      const line = "You " + m[1] + " " + m[2].trim();
      addConsequence(A, line);
      if (/killed|murdered|destroyed|burned/.test(m[1])) addLegacy(A, line);
      msgs.push("Consequence recorded");
    }
  }
  if (/\b(got engaged|were married|won the war|ended the war|became (king|queen|leader))\b/.test(t)) {
    addLegacy(A, text.trim().slice(0, 120));
    msgs.push("Legacy recorded");
  }
  return msgs;
}

function injectStoryExtras(A) {
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  ensureStoryExtras(A);
  let extra = "";
  if (A.sceneAnchor.location) {
    extra += "Current location: " + A.sceneAnchor.location + ". ";
  }
  if (A.sceneAnchor.characters && A.sceneAnchor.characters.length) {
    extra += "Present: " + A.sceneAnchor.characters.join(", ") + ". ";
  }
  if (A.consequences.length) {
    extra += "Consequences to respect: " + A.consequences.slice(-4).join("; ") + ". ";
  }
  if (extra) {
    state.memory.frontMemory = (state.memory.frontMemory || "") + "\n[Anchor] " + extra;
  }
}

function updatePiece24Cards(A) {
  writeCard("Consequence Memory", buildConsequenceCard(A), "· Aetherheart");
  writeCard("Rumor Mill", buildRumorCard(A), "· Aetherheart");
  writeCard("Legacy", buildLegacyCard(A), "· Aetherheart");
  writeCard("Scene Anchor", buildSceneAnchorCard(A), "· Aetherheart");
}

  msgs = msgs.concat(handleScheduleDel(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleInjurySpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleJailSpeech(text, A));

Aetherheart – Piece 25
(Sleeve Jump + Shift + Isekai handlers)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 25: Sleeve Jump, Shift, Isekai handlers
// ============================================================

function ensureJumpState(A) {
  if (!A.sleeve) A.sleeve = null;
  if (!A.shiftState) A.shiftState = { active: false, origin: null, changes: [] };
  if (!A.isekaiState) A.isekaiState = { armed: false, fired: false, originPreset: null, outworldPreset: null };
  return A;
}

function handleSleeveJump(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.sleeveJump) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  if (!/\b(resleeve|new sleeve|sleeve jump|change bodies|download into)\b/.test(t)) return msgs;

  ensureJumpState(A);
  // Keep mental stats, replace physical
  const mental = {
    INT: A.stats.INT,
    WIS: A.stats.WIS,
    CHA: A.stats.CHA
  };
  // Physical reroll baseline (simple)
  A.stats.STR = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7);
  A.stats.DEX = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7);
  A.stats.CON = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7);
  A.stats.INT = mental.INT;
  A.stats.WIS = mental.WIS;
  A.stats.CHA = mental.CHA;
  if (A.vitals) {
    A.vitals.maxHp = 80 + A.stats.CON * 2;
    A.vitals.hp = A.vitals.maxHp;
    A.vitals.maxStamina = 80 + A.stats.CON;
    A.vitals.stamina = A.vitals.maxStamina;
  }
  A.sleeve = { at: (A.time ? A.time.year + "-" + A.time.month + "-" + A.time.day : "now") };
  msgs.push("Sleeve jump complete — physical stats replaced, mental kept");
  return msgs;
}

function handleShift(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.shift) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  ensureJumpState(A);

  if (/\b(time shift|shift (back|forward)|jump (back|forward) in time)\b/.test(t)) {
    if (!A.shiftState.active) {
      A.shiftState.active = true;
      A.shiftState.origin = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(A.time || {}));
      A.shiftState.changes = [];
      msgs.push("Shift started — actions may alter the present on return");
    }
  }
  if (A.shiftState.active && /\b(return to (the )?present|end shift|shift back home)\b/.test(t)) {
    A.shiftState.active = false;
    msgs.push("Returned to main line — timeline updates applied");
    // changes list can drive narrative; state already mutated live
  }
  return msgs;
}

function handleIsekai(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.isekai) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  ensureJumpState(A);

  // Arm / fire displacement
  if (/\b(isekai|transported|summoned to another world|pulled into another world)\b/.test(t)) {
    if (!A.isekaiState.fired) {
      A.isekaiState.fired = true;
      A.isekaiState.originPreset = A.settings.preset;
      msgs.push("Isekai displacement — you remain you in the outworld body");
      addJournalEntry(A, "You were displaced into another world.");
      addLegacy(A, "Displaced into another world.");
    }
  }
  return msgs;
}

function buildJumpStatusCard(A) {
  ensureJumpState(A);
  let lines = ["== JUMP STATUS ==", ""];
  lines.push("Sleeve: " + (A.sleeve ? "active (" + A.sleeve.at + ")" : "none"));
  lines.push("Shift: " + (A.shiftState.active ? "IN PAST/FUTURE" : "main line"));
  lines.push("Isekai: " + (A.isekaiState.fired ? "displaced" : "not fired"));
  lines.push("");
  lines.push("[Sleeve = new body, same mind]");
  lines.push("[Shift = deliberate time jump with return]");
  lines.push("[Isekai = one-way displacement, identity kept]");
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function updatePiece25Cards(A) {
  if (A.settings.sleeveJump || A.settings.shift || A.settings.isekai) {
    writeCard("Jump Status", buildJumpStatusCard(A), "· Aetherheart");
  }
}

function processTurnSystems(text, A) {
  const minutes = parseTimePerTurn(A.time && A.time.timePerTurn);
  advanceTime(A, minutes);
  tickSurvival(A, minutes);
  tickInjuries(A);
  tickHeat(A);
  tickJail(A, minutes);
  readWereMerCard(A);

  let msgs = [];
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleInventoryVerbs(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleBountyActions(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleJobActions(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleRestAndFuel(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleCurrencySpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleXPSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleDrugUse(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleTransformation(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleScheduleDel(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleInjurySpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleJailSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleConsequenceSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleSleeveJump(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleShift(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleIsekai(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(expandNaturalLanguage(text, A));

  if (msgs.length && !A.settings.silentMode) {
    state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
      msgs.map(m => "[SYSTEM] " + m).join("\n");
  }
}

Worked for 5s
Aetherheart – Piece 26
(Ammo + Encumbrance enforcement + Momentum combat + Equip slots)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 26: Ammo, Encumbrance, Momentum combat,
// Equip slots
// ============================================================

function ensureEquip(A) {
  if (!A.equipped) {
    A.equipped = { weapon: null, armor: null, clothes: null };
  }
  if (!A.inventory) {
    A.inventory = { clothes: {}, armor: {}, weapons: {}, ammo: {}, other: {} };
  }
  return A;
}

function handleAmmo(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.ammo) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  ensureEquip(A);

  if (/\b(reload|reload my|load rounds|load shells)\b/.test(t)) {
    const ammoKeys = Object.keys(A.inventory.ammo || {});
    if (ammoKeys.length) {
      const k = ammoKeys[0];
      if ((A.inventory.ammo[k] || 0) > 0) {
        A.inventory.ammo[k] -= 1;
        if (A.inventory.ammo[k] <= 0) delete A.inventory.ammo[k];
        msgs.push("Reloaded using " + k);
      } else msgs.push("No ammo left");
    } else msgs.push("No ammo in inventory");
  }

  if (/\b(shoot|fire|gunshot|empty the clip)\b/.test(t)) {
    const ammoKeys = Object.keys(A.inventory.ammo || {});
    if (ammoKeys.length && (A.inventory.ammo[ammoKeys[0]] || 0) > 0) {
      A.inventory.ammo[ammoKeys[0]] -= 1;
      if (A.inventory.ammo[ammoKeys[0]] <= 0) delete A.inventory.ammo[ammoKeys[0]];
      tickMomentum(A, true);
      msgs.push("Shot fired");
    } else {
      tickMomentum(A, false);
      msgs.push("Click — empty");
    }
  }
  return msgs;
}

function handleEquipSlots(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.inventoryEnabled) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  ensureEquip(A);

  let m = t.match(/\b(?:equip|wear|put on|don)\s+(?:the\s+)?([a-z0-9\s\-']{2,40})/);
  if (m) {
    const item = m[1].trim();
    // naive slot guess
    if (/(armor|vest|mail|plate|helmet|pads)/.test(item)) {
      A.equipped.armor = item;
      msgs.push("Armor equipped: " + item);
    } else if (/(sword|gun|rifle|pistol|bow|knife|blade|staff|hammer|bat)/.test(item)) {
      A.equipped.weapon = item;
      msgs.push("Weapon equipped: " + item);
    } else {
      A.equipped.clothes = item;
      msgs.push("Wearing: " + item);
    }
  }

  m = t.match(/\b(?:unequip|remove|take off)\s+(?:the\s+)?([a-z0-9\s\-']{2,40})/);
  if (m) {
    const item = m[1].trim();
    if (A.equipped.weapon && A.equipped.weapon.indexOf(item) !== -1) A.equipped.weapon = null;
    if (A.equipped.armor && A.equipped.armor.indexOf(item) !== -1) A.equipped.armor = null;
    if (A.equipped.clothes && A.equipped.clothes.indexOf(item) !== -1) A.equipped.clothes = null;
    msgs.push("Removed: " + item);
  }
  return msgs;
}

function enforceEncumbrance(A) {
  const e = calcEncumbrance(A);
  if (!e.overloaded) return [];
  // Overloaded: stamina pressure
  if (A.settings.vitals && A.vitals) {
    A.vitals.stamina = Math.max(0, A.vitals.stamina - 2);
  }
  return ["Overloaded — stamina drained"];
}

function handleCombatMomentum(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.momentum) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  if (/\b(hit|strike|connect|land a blow|critical)\b/.test(t)) {
    tickMomentum(A, true);
    msgs.push("Momentum up");
  }
  if (/\b(miss|fumble|fail|stagger|knocked down)\b/.test(t)) {
    tickMomentum(A, false);
    msgs.push("Momentum down");
  }
  return msgs;
}

function buildEquipCard(A) {
  ensureEquip(A);
  const e = A.equipped;
  const enc = calcEncumbrance(A);
  return `== EQUIPPED ==
Weapon: ${e.weapon || "(none)"}
Armor: ${e.armor || "(none)"}
Clothes: ${e.clothes || "(none)"}

${buildEncumbranceLine(A)}
${A.settings.momentum ? buildMomentumLine(A) : ""}

[Armor and weapons default to permanent (#)]
[Overloaded drains stamina each turn]`;
}

function updatePiece26Cards(A) {
  if (A.settings.inventoryEnabled || A.settings.rpSystem) {
    writeCard("Equipped", buildEquipCard(A), "· Aetherheart (RP System)");
  }
}

function processTurnSystems(text, A) {
  const minutes = parseTimePerTurn(A.time && A.time.timePerTurn);
  advanceTime(A, minutes);
  tickSurvival(A, minutes);
  tickInjuries(A);
  tickHeat(A);
  tickJail(A, minutes);
  readWereMerCard(A);

  let msgs = [];
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleInventoryVerbs(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleEquipSlots(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleAmmo(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleBountyActions(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleJobActions(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleRestAndFuel(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleCurrencySpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleXPSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleDrugUse(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleTransformation(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleScheduleDel(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleInjurySpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleJailSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleConsequenceSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleSleeveJump(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleShift(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleIsekai(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleCombatMomentum(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(enforceEncumbrance(A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(expandNaturalLanguage(text, A));

  if (msgs.length && !A.settings.silentMode) {
    state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
      msgs.map(m => "[SYSTEM] " + m).join("\n");
  }
}

Worked for 2s
Aetherheart – Piece 27
(Story Splice activation + Speech injection + Narration flags)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 27: Story Splice activation, Speech
// pattern injection, Narration flag readers
// ============================================================

function ensureNarrationState(A) {
  if (!A.splices) A.splices = [];
  if (!A.settings.narratePlayer) A.settings.narratePlayer = false;
  if (!A.settings.presentTense) A.settings.presentTense = true;
  if (!A.settings.dynamicPacing) A.settings.dynamicPacing = false;
  if (!A.settings.controlledProse) A.settings.controlledProse = true;
  if (!A.settings.limitBadHabits) A.settings.limitBadHabits = true;
  if (!A.settings.speechPatterns) A.settings.speechPatterns = false;
  return A;
}

function readNarrationCard(A) {
  const card = findCard("Narration");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  const e = card.entry;
  const pairs = [
    ["Narrate Player", "narratePlayer"],
    ["Present Tense", "presentTense"],
    ["Dynamic Pacing", "dynamicPacing"],
    ["Controlled Prose", "controlledProse"],
    ["Limit Bad Habits", "limitBadHabits"],
    ["Speech Patterns", "speechPatterns"],
    ["Localized Languages", "localizedLanguages"],
    ["Jailbreak", "jailbreak"],
    ["Story Splice", "storySplice"]
  ];
  pairs.forEach(([label, key]) => {
    const v = parseOnOff(e, label);
    if (v !== null) A.settings[key] = v;
  });
}

function handleSpliceCommands(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.storySplice) return [];
  const msgs = [];
  ensureNarrationState(A);
  let m = text.match(/\[SPLICE ON:\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
  if (m) {
    const name = m[1].trim();
    if (A.splices.indexOf(name) === -1) A.splices.push(name);
    msgs.push("Splice on: " + name);
  }
  m = text.match(/\[SPLICE OFF:\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
  if (m) {
    const name = m[1].trim();
    A.splices = A.splices.filter(s => s.toLowerCase() !== name.toLowerCase());
    msgs.push("Splice off: " + name);
  }
  if (/\[SPLICE CLEAR\]/i.test(text)) {
    A.splices = [];
    msgs.push("All splices cleared");
  }
  return msgs;
}

function buildActiveSplicesLine(A) {
  ensureNarrationState(A);
  if (!A.splices.length) return "Active Splices: (none)";
  return "Active Splices: " + A.splices.join(", ");
}

function injectNarrationBias(A) {
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  ensureNarrationState(A);
  let bias = [];

  if (A.settings.narratePlayer) {
    bias.push("Narrate NPCs only; do not write the player character's actions or dialogue.");
  }
  if (A.settings.presentTense) {
    bias.push("Write in present tense.");
  } else {
    bias.push("Write in past tense.");
  }
  if (A.settings.controlledProse) {
    bias.push("Avoid purple prose. Keep descriptions concrete.");
  }
  if (A.settings.limitBadHabits) {
    bias.push("Do not spam psychic aftershocks, glowing sentient objects, or energy with feelings.");
  }
  if (A.settings.dynamicPacing) {
    bias.push("Speed up during action; slow down during quiet, romance, or reflection.");
  }
  if (A.settings.storySplice && A.splices && A.splices.length) {
    bias.push("Apply narrative flavor only from these splices: " + A.splices.join(", ") + ". Do not change plot.");
  }
  if (A.settings.jailbreak) {
    bias.push("Jailbreak active: suspend normal story and persona constraints for troubleshooting.");
  }

  // Character speech patterns from cards
  if (A.settings.speechPatterns && Array.isArray(storyCards)) {
    storyCards.forEach(c => {
      if (!c || !c.entry) return;
      const m = c.entry.match(/Speech:\s*([^\n]+)/i);
      if (m) {
        bias.push(c.title + " must speak in this pattern: " + m[1].trim() + ".");
      }
    });
  }

  if (bias.length) {
    state.memory.authorsNote = (state.memory.authorsNote ? state.memory.authorsNote + "\n" : "") +
      bias.join(" ");
  }
}

function updateStorySpliceCard(A) {
  if (!A.settings.storySplice) return;
  const base = buildStorySpliceCard(A);
  const active = buildActiveSplicesLine(A);
  writeCard("Story Splice", active + "\n\n" + base +
    "\n\n[SPLICE ON: Name]\n[SPLICE OFF: Name]\n[SPLICE CLEAR]",
    "· Aetherheart (Narration)");
}

function updatePiece27Cards(A) {
  if (A.settings.narrationSystem) {
    writeCard("Narration", buildNarrationCard(A), "· Aetherheart (Narration)");
  }
  updateStorySpliceCard(A);
}

  msgs = msgs.concat(handleCurrencySpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleXPSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleDrugUse(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleTransformation(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleScheduleDel(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleInjurySpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleJailSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleConsequenceSpeech(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleSleeveJump(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleShift(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleIsekai(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleCombatMomentum(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(handleSpliceCommands(text, A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(enforceEncumbrance(A));
  msgs = msgs.concat(expandNaturalLanguage(text, A));

  if (msgs.length && !A.settings.silentMode) {
    state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") +
      msgs.map(m => "[SYSTEM] " + m).join("\n");
  }

Worked for 5s
Aetherheart – Piece 28
(Confessional Cutaway + Deadpool break + Bombastic pose)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 28: Confessional Cutaway, Deadpool break,
// Bombastic Superhero pose
// ============================================================

function ensureNarrativeDevices(A) {
  if (!A.devices) {
    A.devices = {
      confessionalCooldown: 0,
      deadpoolCooldown: 0,
      deadpoolEvery: 8, // randomized later
      lastConfessionalTurn: -999,
      turn: 0
    };
  }
  return A.devices;
}

function rollDeadpoolInterval() {
  return 5 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10); // 5–14 turns
}

function wantsConfessional(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.storySplice) return false;
  if (!A.splices || A.splices.indexOf("Confessional Cutaway") === -1) return false;
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  // high comedy / tension cues
  return /\b(what the|can you believe|i can't believe|this is insane|humiliated|caught|walked in on|awkward|unbelievable)\b/.test(t);
}

function wantsDeadpool(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.storySplice) return false;
  if (!A.splices || A.splices.indexOf("Deadpool Break") === -1) return false;
  const d = ensureNarrativeDevices(A);
  if (d.deadpoolCooldown > 0) return false;
  // player-only break — triggered by addressing reader / meta
  return /\b(look at the camera|aside to the reader|breaking the fourth wall|hey reader|hey audience)\b/.test(text.toLowerCase()) ||
    Math.random() < 0.08;
}

function wantsBombasticPose(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.storySplice) return false;
  if (!A.splices || A.splices.indexOf("Bombastic Superhero") === -1) return false;
  // declarative statements often end with ! or justice/hero language
  return /!/.test(text) || /\b(justice|declare|vow|shall|must)\b/i.test(text);
}

function injectDeviceBias(A, text) {
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  const d = ensureNarrativeDevices(A);
  d.turn = (d.turn || 0) + 1;
  if (d.deadpoolCooldown > 0) d.deadpoolCooldown--;
  if (d.confessionalCooldown > 0) d.confessionalCooldown--;

  let bits = [];

  if (wantsConfessional(text, A) && d.confessionalCooldown <= 0) {
    bits.push("If tension or comedy is high, insert one Confessional Cutaway: suspend the scene, max two sentences of the character settling into a private booth, then they speak directly to the reader about their real feelings, then snap back to the exact same moment. The character does not walk off or walk back on.");
    d.confessionalCooldown = 6;
  }

  if (wantsDeadpool(text, A)) {
    bits.push("Allow one Deadpool-style fourth-wall break from the player character only: they stay in the scene and speak directly to the reader. Then continue normally.");
    d.deadpoolCooldown = d.deadpoolEvery || rollDeadpoolInterval();
    d.deadpoolEvery = rollDeadpoolInterval();
  }

  if (wantsBombasticPose(text, A)) {
    bits.push("On each declarative statement by a Bombastic Superhero character, briefly describe a Superman pose looking into the distance (cape billows if present). Keep it short.");
  }

  if (A.splices && A.splices.indexOf("Live Studio Audience") !== -1) {
    bits.push("Use live studio audience reactions sparingly: laugh tracks, gasps, oooohs — narrative flavor only.");
  }

  if (A.splices && A.splices.indexOf("Full Inner Monologue") !== -1) {
    bits.push("All narration is the POV character's inner monologue. No external narrator voice.");
  }

  if (bits.length) {
    state.memory.frontMemory = (state.memory.frontMemory || "") + "\n[Devices] " + bits.join(" ");
  }
}

function buildDevicesCard(A) {
  const d = ensureNarrativeDevices(A);
  return `== NARRATIVE DEVICES ==
Confessional cooldown: ${d.confessionalCooldown || 0}
Deadpool cooldown: ${d.deadpoolCooldown || 0}
Next Deadpool window: ~${d.deadpoolEvery || 8} turns

Active Splices drive these devices.
Confessional Cutaway = out-of-scene booth, snap back.
Deadpool Break = in-scene, player only, random cooldown.
Bombastic Superhero = pose on declarative lines.
Live Studio Audience = reactions only.
Full Inner Monologue = entire narration is inner voice.

[Style only — never changes plot]`;
}

function updatePiece28Cards(A) {
  if (A.settings.storySplice) {
    writeCard("Narrative Devices", buildDevicesCard(A), "· Aetherheart (Narration)");
  }
}

function onOutput_Aetherheart(text) {
  const A = ensureState();
  if (!A.settings.scriptEnabled) return text;

  syncFromCards(A);
  readNarrationCard(A);
  text = filterBannedPhrases(text);
  applyStandardRules(A);
  injectNarrationBias(A);
  injectDeviceBias(A, text);
  processBills(A);
  injectFrontMemory(A);
  injectStoryExtras(A);
  scanLocationAndShiftCards(A);
  syncScheduleDayCards(A);

  updateAllCards(A);
  updatePiece11Cards(A);
  updatePiece12Cards(A);
  updatePiece13Cards(A);
  updatePiece14Cards(A);
  updatePiece18Cards(A);
  updatePiece19Cards(A);
  buildEventsAndArc(A);
  updatePiece21Cards(A);
  updatePiece23Cards(A);
  updatePiece24Cards(A);
  updatePiece25Cards(A);
  updatePiece26Cards(A);
  updatePiece27Cards(A);
  updatePiece28Cards(A);

  if (checkGameOver(A)) text = buildGameOverScreen();
  return text;
}

Confessional Cutaway (dynamic, cooldown, exact booth rules)
Deadpool break (player only, random cooldown)
Bombastic Superhero pose on declarative lines
Live Studio Audience + Full Inner Monologue bias
Narrative Devices status card

Aetherheart – Piece 29
(Information Firewall enforcement + API card + Silent Mode messages)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 29: Firewall, API/Data Source,
// Silent Mode message bus
// ============================================================

function ensureQoLState(A) {
  if (!A.messages) A.messages = [];
  if (!A.settings.informationFirewall) A.settings.informationFirewall = true;
  return A;
}

function pushSystemMessage(A, msg) {
  ensureQoLState(A);
  if (A.settings.silentMode) {
    // store for popup-style delivery via state.message only when asked
    A.messages.push(msg);
    if (A.messages.length > 20) A.messages = A.messages.slice(-15);
  } else {
    state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") + "[SYSTEM] " + msg;
  }
}

function flushSilentMessages(A) {
  if (!A.settings.silentMode) return;
  if (A.messages && A.messages.length) {
    state.message = A.messages.map(m => "[SYSTEM] " + m).join("\n");
    A.messages = [];
  }
}

function injectFirewall(A) {
  if (!A.settings.informationFirewall) return;
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  const rule =
    "Information Firewall: characters only know what they have experienced or been told in-world. " +
    "No meta knowledge of stats, menus, script systems, or out-of-scene events.";
  state.memory.authorsNote = (state.memory.authorsNote ? state.memory.authorsNote + "\n" : "") + rule;
}

function readAPICard(A) {
  if (!A.settings.api) return;
  const card = findCard("Data Source");
  if (!card) return;
  // Notes field holds the single website; entry stays blank/unlabeled
  // Script only records presence for future hooks
  A.dataSource = {
    present: true,
    notes: card.entry || ""
  };
}

function injectDataSourceNudge(A) {
  if (!A.settings.api || !A.dataSource || !A.dataSource.present) return;
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  // Conservative: nudge model to treat known lore as authoritative if it already has it
  state.memory.frontMemory = (state.memory.frontMemory || "") +
    "\n[Data] Prefer established franchise/world facts when consistent with this adventure.";
}

function handleStatusCommands(text, A) {
  const cmd = text.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (cmd === "/status") {
    flushSilentMessages(A);
    state.message = buildStatusCard(A);
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd === "/messages" && A.settings.silentMode) {
    flushSilentMessages(A);
    if (!state.message) state.message = "[SYSTEM] No queued messages.";
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// Rewrite processTurnSystems message path to use pushSystemMessage
function emitMsgs(A, msgs) {
  if (!msgs || !msgs.length) return;
  msgs.forEach(m => pushSystemMessage(A, m));
}

function processTurnSystems(text, A) {
  const minutes = parseTimePerTurn(A.time && A.time.timePerTurn);
  advanceTime(A, minutes);
  tickSurvival(A, minutes);
  tickInjuries(A);
  tickHeat(A);
  tickJail(A, minutes);
  readWereMerCard(A);
  readNarrationCard(A);
  readAPICard(A);

  let msgs = [];

function onInput_Aetherheart(text) {
  const A = ensureState();
  syncFromCards(A);
  if (!A.settings.scriptEnabled) return text;

  if (handleResetCommands(text, A)) return text;
  if (handleSaveLoadCommands(text, A)) return text;
  if (handleCardCommand(text, A)) return text;
  if (handleStatusCommands(text, A)) return text;

  const addMsgs = handleAddCommands(text, A);
  emitMsgs(A, addMsgs);

  const d20Result = handleD20(text, A);
  if (d20Result) pushSystemMessage(A, d20Result.replace(/^\[d20\]\s*/, "d20 "));

  processTurnSystems(text, A);
  maybeLootLetter(A, text);
  compressThoughts(A);
  syncListsFromCards(A);
  autoTitleExistingCards(A);
  scanLocationAndShiftCards(A);

  const cmd = text.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (cmd === "/help") state.message = buildHelpCard();
  if (cmd === "/keywords") state.message = buildKeywordsCard();
  if (cmd === "/jailbreak") {
    A.settings.jailbreak = true;
    pushSystemMessage(A, "Jailbreak active.");
  }
  if (cmd === "/balance") pushSystemMessage(A, "Balance: " + (A.balance || 0));
  if (cmd === "/inventory") state.message = buildInventoryCard(A);
  if (cmd === "/time") {
    const t = A.time || {};
    pushSystemMessage(A, getMonthName(t.month) + " " + t.day + ", Year " + t.year + " | " + formatHour(t.hour));
  }

  return text;
}

Aetherheart – Piece 30
(Quest progress + Bounty expiry + Card cleanup + Help refresh)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 30: Quest progress, Bounty expiry,
// Card cleanup, Help refresh
// ============================================================

function handleQuestProgress(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.quests || !A.quests || !A.quests.length) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];

  if (/\b(quest (complete|done|finished)|completed the quest)\b/.test(t)) {
    const q = A.quests[0];
    if (q) {
      q.progress = 100;
      msgs.push("Quest complete: " + q.title);
      gainXP(A, 100);
      addJournalEntry(A, "Quest completed: " + q.title);
      A.quests.shift();
    }
  }

  // light progress bump on effort language
  if (/\b(made progress|got closer|found a clue|tracked them down)\b/.test(t)) {
    A.quests[0].progress = Math.min(100, (A.quests[0].progress || 0) + 15);
    msgs.push("Quest progress: " + A.quests[0].title + " " + A.quests[0].progress + "%");
  }
  return msgs;
}

function tickBountyExpiry(A) {
  if (!A.bounties || !A.bounties.length) return;
  A.bounties.forEach(b => {
    if (b.expiresIn == null) return;
    b.expiresIn -= 1;
  });
  A.bounties = A.bounties.filter(b => b.expiresIn == null || b.expiresIn > 0);
}

function cleanupEmptyAetherCards() {
  if (!Array.isArray(storyCards)) return;
  // Remove truly empty Aetherheart helper cards that were wiped by user
  // Keep Configure and format cards
  for (let i = storyCards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const c = storyCards[i];
    if (!c || !c.title) continue;
    if (c.title === "Configure Aetherheart") continue;
    if (c.title.indexOf("_") === 0) continue;
    if (c.entry != null && String(c.entry).trim() === "" && c.title.indexOf("Aether") === -1) {
      // leave user cards alone
    }
  }
}

function buildHelpCard() {
  return `== HELP ==
Aetherheart is modular. Turn systems ON from Configure Aetherheart.
Most actions work by natural language.

Core
/help  /keywords  /status  /save  /load  /jailbreak
/reset memory  /reset all
/balance  /inventory  /time  /messages

Boards
check the bounty board | take the bounty on X | turn in the bounty
check the job board | take the job | complete the job

Inventory
give / take / equip / unequip / reload / buy

Time & Survival
rest | sleep | refuel
Schedule and holidays enforce when ON

RP
Roll for Attack / Saving / Ability Check (name) / Initiative
Level up from combat and quests

Jumps
resleeve | time shift | return to present | isekai

Splices
[SPLICE ON: Name] [SPLICE OFF: Name] [SPLICE CLEAR]

Cards
/card Character Title details
/card Location Title details

Silent Mode queues system messages — use /messages or /status.
Data is kept when systems turn OFF.
[Full keyword list on Keywords card]`;
}

function buildKeywordsCard() {
  return `== KEYWORDS ==
No triggers. Reference only.

/ah enable|disable|toggle
/help /keywords /status /save /load /jailbreak
/reset memory /reset all
/balance /inventory /time /messages
/stats /abilities /powers /journal
/bounty /jobs /quests /weather /schedule

Natural language covers almost all of the above.
Silent Mode: system text becomes queued popups.
[SPLICE ON/OFF/CLEAR] for narrative flavor only.`;
}

Aetherheart – Piece 31
(Permanence # + Rating bias + /ah + Currency parse + Fuel travel)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 31: # permanence, Rating, /ah,
// Currency parse, Fuel travel
// ============================================================

function isPermanentName(name) {
  return /#/.test(name) || /\b(armor|sword|gun|rifle|pistol|blade|vest|plate|helmet)\b/i.test(name);
}

function stripHash(name) {
  return String(name).replace(/#/g, "").trim();
}

function inventoryAdd(A, category, name, qty) {
  if (!A.inventory) A.inventory = { clothes: {}, armor: {}, weapons: {}, ammo: {}, other: {} };
  if (!A.inventory[category]) A.inventory[category] = {};
  const permanent = isPermanentName(name);
  const clean = stripHash(name);
  const q = permanent ? 1 : (qty || 1);
  if (permanent) {
    A.inventory[category][clean + " #"] = 1;
  } else {
    A.inventory[category][clean] = (A.inventory[category][clean] || 0) + q;
  }
}

function readCurrencyConfig(A) {
  const card = findCard("Configure Aetherheart");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  const name = parseValue(card.entry, "Currency name");
  const sym = parseValue(card.entry, "Currency symbol");
  if (name) A.currencyName = name.split(/\n/)[0].trim();
  if (sym) A.currencySymbol = sym.split(/\n/)[0].trim();
}

function injectRating(A) {
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  const r = (A.settings.rating || "E").toUpperCase();
  let line = "Content rating: " + r + ". ";
  if (r === "E") line += "Keep content everyone-friendly.";
  else if (r === "TEEN" || r === "T") line += "Teen-level content only.";
  else if (r === "NSFW" || r === "M") line += "Mature content allowed.";
  else line += "Unrated — no holds barred.";
  state.memory.authorsNote = (state.memory.authorsNote ? state.memory.authorsNote + "\n" : "") + line;
}

function handleAhCommand(text, A) {
  const m = text.trim().match(/^\/ah\s+(enable|disable|toggle|inv|currency|time|bills|cards)\s*(on|off)?$/i);
  if (!m) return false;
  const act = m[1].toLowerCase();
  const arg = (m[2] || "").toLowerCase();
  if (act === "enable") A.settings.scriptEnabled = true;
  else if (act === "disable") A.settings.scriptEnabled = false;
  else if (act === "toggle") A.settings.scriptEnabled = !A.settings.scriptEnabled;
  else if (act === "inv") A.settings.inventoryEnabled = arg !== "off";
  else if (act === "currency") A.settings.currencyEnabled = arg !== "off";
  else if (act === "time") A.settings.timeEnabled = arg !== "off";
  else if (act === "bills") A.settings.billsEnabled = arg !== "off";
  else if (act === "cards") A.settings.cardCreator = arg !== "off";
  pushSystemMessage(A, "Aetherheart: " + act + (arg ? " " + arg : "") + " applied");
  return true;
}

function handleTravel(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.fuel && !A.settings.timeEnabled) return [];
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const m = t.match(/\b(?:travel|drive|ride|fly|go)\s+to\s+([a-z0-9\s\-']{2,40})/);
  if (!m) return [];
  const dest = m[1].trim();
  const msgs = [];
  // find any route containing dest
  const routes = A.routes || {};
  let spent = 10;
  Object.keys(routes).forEach(k => {
    if (k.toLowerCase().indexOf(dest.toLowerCase()) !== -1) {
      const n = parseInt(String(routes[k]).replace(/[^\d]/g, ""), 10);
      if (!isNaN(n)) spent = Math.min(50, Math.max(5, Math.floor(n / 10)));
    }
  });
  if (A.settings.fuel) {
    A.fuel = Math.max(0, (A.fuel || 100) - spent);
    msgs.push("Traveled toward " + dest + " (-" + spent + "% " + (A.settings.fuelName || "Fuel") + ")");
  } else {
    msgs.push("Traveled toward " + dest);
  }
  advanceTime(A, spent * 6);
  if (A.sceneAnchor) A.sceneAnchor.location = dest;
  return msgs;
}

function safeEncode(str) {
  try {
    if (typeof btoa === "function") return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(str)));
  } catch (e) {}
  // fallback: compact URI component
  try { return encodeURIComponent(str); } catch (e2) { return str; }
}

function safeDecode(str) {
  try {
    if (typeof atob === "function") return decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(str)));
  } catch (e) {}
  try { return decodeURIComponent(str); } catch (e2) { return null; }
}

function encodeSaveState(A) {
  try {
    const payload = {
      v: AETHER_VERSION,
      level: A.level, xp: A.xp, stats: A.stats, vitals: A.vitals,
      survival: A.survival, balance: A.balance, reputation: A.reputation,
      inventory: A.inventory, journal: (A.journal || []).slice(-20),
      quests: A.quests, time: A.time,
      settings: { preset: A.settings.preset, rating: A.settings.rating, magicMode: A.settings.magicMode }
    };
    return safeEncode(JSON.stringify(payload));
  } catch (e) { return "SAVE_ERROR"; }
}

function decodeSaveState(block) {
  try {
    const raw = safeDecode(block.trim());
    if (!raw) return null;
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (e) { return null; }
}
Aetherheart – Piece 32
(User Rules cards + Essentials supplement + Drug duration + Holidays check)

// ============================================================
// Aetherheart - Piece 32: User Rules, Essentials supplement,
// Drug duration, Holiday active check
// ============================================================

function collectUserRules() {
  if (!Array.isArray(storyCards)) return [];
  const rules = [];
  storyCards.forEach(c => {
    if (!c || !c.title) return;
    if (/^Aetherheart Rules/i.test(c.title)) {
      if (c.entry) rules.push(c.entry.trim());
    }
  });
  return rules;
}

function injectUserRules(A) {
  const rules = collectUserRules();
  if (!rules.length) return;
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  state.memory.authorsNote = (state.memory.authorsNote ? state.memory.authorsNote + "\n" : "") +
    rules.join("\n");
}

function injectSceneSupplement(A) {
  if (!A.settings.essentialsSupplement && !A.settings.currencyContext) return;
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  let extra = [];
  if (A.settings.vitals && A.vitals) {
    const v = A.vitals;
    extra.push("HP " + v.hp + "/" + v.maxHp + " MP " + v.mp + "/" + v.maxMp + " STA " + v.stamina + "/" + v.maxStamina);
  }
  if (A.settings.leveling) extra.push("Level " + A.level);
  if (A.settings.currencyContext && A.settings.currencyEnabled) {
    extra.push("Balance " + (A.currencySymbol || "$") + (A.balance || 0));
  }
  if (extra.length) {
    state.memory.context = (state.memory.context || "") + "\n[Aetherheart] " + extra.join(" | ");
  }
}

function ensureDrugTimers(A) {
  if (!A.drugTimers) A.drugTimers = [];
  return A.drugTimers;
}

function tickDrugTimers(A) {
  const timers = ensureDrugTimers(A);
  const keep = [];
  timers.forEach(d => {
    d.turnsLeft = (d.turnsLeft || 1) - 1;
    if (d.turnsLeft > 0) keep.push(d);
  });
  A.drugTimers = keep;
}

function handleDrugUse(text, A) {
  const t = text.toLowerCase();
  const msgs = [];
  if (/\b(smoke|hit|use)\b.*\b(blunt|joint|weed)\b/.test(t) ||
      /\b(blunt|joint|weed)\b.*\b(smoke|hit|use)\b/.test(t)) {
    if (A.settings.survival) {
      A.survival.hunger = Math.min(100, (A.survival.hunger || 0) + 20);
      A.survival.thirst = Math.min(100, (A.survival.thirst || 0) + 40);
      A.survival.tiredness = Math.min(100, (A.survival.tiredness || 0) + 30);
    }
    if (A.settings.vitals && A.vitals) {
      A.vitals.stamina = Math.max(0, A.vitals.stamina - 10);
    }
    ensureDrugTimers(A).push({ name: "weed", turnsLeft: 4 });
    msgs.push("Used weed (duration active)");
  }
  return msgs;
}

function readHolidaysActive(A) {
  if (!A.settings.timeEnabled) return [];
  const card = findCard("Holidays");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return [];
  const t = A.time || {};
  const active = [];
  const lines = card.entry.split(/\n/);
  lines.forEach(line => {
    // Name | Month Day
    const m = line.match(/^\s*([^|]+)\|\s*(\d+)\s+(\d+)/);
    if (m) {
      const month = parseInt(m[2], 10);
      const day = parseInt(m[3], 10);
      if (month === t.month && day === t.day) active.push(m[1].trim());
    }
  });
  return active;
}

function injectHoliday(A) {
  const active = readHolidaysActive(A);
  if (!active.length) return;
  if (!state.memory) state.memory = {};
  state.memory.frontMemory = (state.memory.frontMemory || "") +
    "\n[Holiday] Today is " + active.join(", ") + ". Reflect this in the world.";
}

  emitMsgs(A, msgs);
}

function onInput_Aetherheart(text) {
  const A = ensureState();
  syncFromCards(A);
  if (!A.settings.scriptEnabled) {
    if (handleAhCommand(text, A)) return text;
    return text;
  }
  if (handleAhCommand(text, A)) return text;
  if (handleResetCommands(text, A)) return text;
  if (handleSaveLoadCommands(text, A)) return text;
  if (handleCardCommand(text, A)) return text;
  if (handleStatusCommands(text, A)) return text;

  emitMsgs(A, handleAddCommands(text, A));
  const d20Result = handleD20(text, A);
  if (d20Result) pushSystemMessage(A, d20Result.replace(/^\[d20\]\s*/, "d20 "));

  processTurnSystems(text, A);
  maybeLootLetter(A, text);
  compressThoughts(A);
  syncListsFromCards(A);
  autoTitleExistingCards(A);
  scanLocationAndShiftCards(A);

  const cmd = text.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (cmd === "/help") state.message = buildHelpCard();
  if (cmd === "/keywords") state.message = buildKeywordsCard();
  if (cmd === "/jailbreak") { A.settings.jailbreak = true; pushSystemMessage(A, "Jailbreak active."); }
  if (cmd === "/balance") pushSystemMessage(A, "Balance: " + (A.currencySymbol || "$") + (A.balance || 0));
  if (cmd === "/inventory") state.message = buildInventoryCard(A);
  if (cmd === "/time") {
    const t = A.time || {};
    pushSystemMessage(A, getMonthName(t.month) + " " + t.day + ", Year " + t.year + " | " + formatHour(t.hour));
  }
  return text;
}

function onOutput_Aetherheart(text) {
  const A = ensureState();
  if (!A.settings.scriptEnabled) return text;

  syncFromCards(A);
  readNarrationCard(A);
  readAPICard(A);
  readCurrencyConfig(A);
  text = filterBannedPhrases(text);
  applyStandardRules(A);
  injectUserRules(A);
  injectRating(A);
  injectFirewall(A);
  injectNarrationBias(A);
  injectDeviceBias(A, text);
  injectDataSourceNudge(A);
  injectHoliday(A);
  processBills(A);
  injectFrontMemory(A);
  injectStoryExtras(A);
  injectSceneSupplement(A);
  scanLocationAndShiftCards(A);
  syncScheduleDayCards(A);

  updateAllCards(A);
  updatePiece11Cards(A);
  updatePiece12Cards(A);
  updatePiece13Cards(A);
  updatePiece14Cards(A);
  updatePiece18Cards(A);
  updatePiece19Cards(A);
  buildEventsAndArc(A);
  updatePiece21Cards(A);
  updatePiece23Cards(A);
  updatePiece24Cards(A);
  updatePiece25Cards(A);
  updatePiece26Cards(A);
  updatePiece27Cards(A);
  updatePiece28Cards(A);
  writeCard("Help", buildHelpCard(), "· Aetherheart");
  writeCard("Keywords", buildKeywordsCard(), "· Aetherheart");

  if (checkGameOver(A)) text = buildGameOverScreen();
  return text;
}

function onLibrary_Aetherheart() {
  const A = ensureState();
  syncFromCards(A);
  updateAllCards(A);
  updatePiece11Cards(A);
  updatePiece12Cards(A);
  updatePiece13Cards(A);
  updatePiece14Cards(A);
  updatePiece18Cards(A);
  updatePiece19Cards(A);
  buildEventsAndArc(A);
  updatePiece21Cards(A);
  updatePiece23Cards(A);
  updatePiece24Cards(A);
  updatePiece25Cards(A);
  updatePiece26Cards(A);
  updatePiece27Cards(A);
  updatePiece28Cards(A);
  writeCard("Help", buildHelpCard(), "· Aetherheart");
  writeCard("Keywords", buildKeywordsCard(), "· Aetherheart");
}

// ==== AI Dungeon entry points ====
function modifier_input(text) { return onInput_Aetherheart(text); }
function modifier_output(text) { return onOutput_Aetherheart(text); }
function modifier_library(text) { onLibrary_Aetherheart(); return text; }

// Boot
onLibrary_Aetherheart();
