Worked for 3s
GitHub crash course (only what you need)

What GitHub is
A website that stores your code online, with version history. Free for public or private repos.

One-time setup
Go to github.com and create an account
On your laptop, install Git (if you don’t have it):
Windows: download Git from git-scm.com
Mac: in Terminal run xcode-select --install or install Git
Optional but nice: install GitHub Desktop (gui, less typing) from desktop.github.com
Create the repo (on the website)
Log in → click New repository
Name it e.g. Aetherheart
Public or Private (your call)
Don’t check “add README” if you already have files (or do — either works)
Click Create repository
Put your files up
Option A — GitHub Desktop (easiest)

Open GitHub Desktop
File → Add Local Repository (or Clone if empty)
Point it at the folder with your text files
Write a short summary: Initial Aetherheart script
Click Commit → Push origin
Option B — Command line
In the folder with your files:

git init
git add .
git commit -m "Initial Aetherheart script"
git branch -M main
git remote add origin https://github.com/YOURUSERNAME/Aetherheart.git
git push -u origin main
(Use the URL GitHub shows you after creating the repo.)

After it’s up
The repo page is your share link
To update later: change files → commit → push
Keep Realmheart copyright notices in the files
What to put in the folder today
Library.js (or .txt) — all Library pieces merged
Input.js
Output.js
Optional: README.md with install steps (Input / Output / Library tabs in AI Dungeon)

Two manuals locked in.

1. Basic Manual (Realmheart-style — short)
🐉🔮 AETHERHEART 🔮🐉
Quick Guide

Aetherheart sits on Realmheart’s modular bones.
Turn systems ON in Configure Aetherheart. Most actions work by normal speech.

────────────────────────────────
CORE
🐉 Configure Aetherheart — master on/off + preset + rating
⏰ Time / Weather / Holidays / Schedule
💰 Currency, Ledger, Prices & Goods, Menu
🎒 Inventory (Clothes / Armor / Weapons / Ammo / Other)

RP
❤️ Vitals & Survival (HP MP Stamina, hunger/thirst/tired)
 comb Leveling, Skills, Abilities, Powers, Loot, d20
🔥 Fuel (renameable), Ammo, Momentum
⚖️ Reputation, Heat, Jail, Bounties → Quests
🐺 Were/Mer transformations

CHARACTERS
🧠 Inner Thoughts — memory notes on cards ({title: Name})
💭 Inner Opinions — relationships, vibe, social pressure
🃏 Card Creator — builds cards only
📖 Player Journal — short “you did X” lines

STORY
🎭 Narration + Story Splices (flavor only)
🗣️ Speech patterns on character cards
📜 Quests, Events, Story Arc
🌀 Sleeve Jump / Shift / Isekai
📍 Scene Anchor, Consequences, Rumors, Legacy

QoL
🔇 Silent Mode, /save /load, /status, /help
🛡️ Information Firewall, Standard Rules
💀 Game Over screen

────────────────────────────────
Commands worth knowing
/help  /keywords  /status  /save  /load
/balance  /inventory  /time  /jailbreak
/reset memory  /reset all

Natural language works for boards, inventory, rest, travel, bounties, jobs.

Data stays when you turn a system OFF.
Containers sort under: 🐉🔮 AetherHeart 🔮🐉
2. Comprehensive Manual (Explain-it-like-I’m-five)
🐉🔮 AETHERHEART — FULL MANUAL (ELI5)
🔮🐉

WHAT IS THIS?
Aetherheart is a big toolbox for AI Dungeon.
Each tool is a system you can turn ON or OFF.
If it’s OFF, it doesn’t bother you.
If it’s ON, it helps the story keep track of things
(time, money, health, quests, memories, and more).

You do not need all of it.
Start with Configure Aetherheart.
Flip on only what your story needs.

────────────────────────────────
1. THE MASTER SWITCH
Card: Configure Aetherheart

This is the control panel.
- Preset = what kind of world (fantasy, western, modern, cyberpunk, sci-fi, wasteland…)
- Rating = how mature the story may be (E, Teen, NSFW, Unrated)
- Rows of ON/OFF for big systems

Change a line from OFF to ON.
On the next turn, Aetherheart creates the cards for that system.

────────────────────────────────
2. TIME ⏰
Keeps a calendar and clock.
Each turn can move minutes or hours (Time/Turn).
Weather and seasons can follow the calendar.
Holidays can be listed; on that day the story knows it’s a holiday.
Schedule lets you set blocks like “Monday breakfast 7:00–7:55”.
Priority: Holiday beats a normal day; a specific day beats “every weekday”.

You can also put hours on a location card:
{hours: 6:00 AM | 5:00 PM}
And shifts on a character card:
{shift: Tavern | 8:00 PM | 2:00 AM}

────────────────────────────────
3. MONEY 💰
Currency name and symbol live on the Configure card.
Ledger remembers what you gained and spent.
You can say things like “buy a sword for 50” or “check my balance”.
Menu/price lists appear when you check prices or look at a menu
(story pauses and shows a list that fits the place you’re in).
Drugs you configure on the main card also show up in Prices & Goods.

────────────────────────────────
4. STUFF YOU CARRY 🎒
Inventory is split so cards don’t get huge:
- Clothes
- Armor
- Weapons
- Ammo
- Other

Armor and weapons are permanent by default (#).
You can mark anything permanent with #.
Only total weight matters for “are you overloaded?”
Overloaded can tire you out.

Say: give, take, equip, unequip, reload, wear, remove.

────────────────────────────────
5. BODY & SURVIVAL ❤️
Vitals: HP, MP, Stamina
Survival: Hunger, Thirst, Tiredness

Resting can restore you and push time forward.
Some magic uses MP (sorcery).
Some magic is words only (wizarding) — good for Harry Potter style.

Injuries can be recorded and slowly fade.
Bad ones may leave scars.

────────────────────────────────
6. GROWING STRONGER ⭐
Leveling tracks XP and level.
Skills, Abilities, Powers can be listed and added.
Loot can get stronger names as you level
(Common → Uncommon → Rare → Epic → Legendary → Mythic).
The strong word always stays at the front of the item name.

d20: say “Roll for Attack” or “Roll for Saving” etc.
Stat % modifiers can boost those rolls.

────────────────────────────────
7. FIGHTING & PRESSURE ⚔️
Ammo is used when you shoot or reload.
Momentum rises when you succeed and falls when you fail
(optional light combat feel).
Deus Ex = intentional god mode (everything bends for you).
Anti-Deus Ex = no cheap sudden rescues when you want it hard.

────────────────────────────────
8. BOUNTIES, JOBS, QUESTS 📜
Bounty Board = wanted posters (dead / alive / either).
Taking a bounty can create a Quest automatically.
Job Board = normal work (deliveries, shifts, shop work).
Talk normally: “check the bounty board”, “take the job”, “turn in the bounty”.

────────────────────────────────
9. REPUTATION & JAIL ⚖️
Law / Social / Heat can move with your deeds.
Heat can fade slowly.
Jail can use the time system (hours tick down).
Bounties on you are optional.

────────────────────────────────
10. MEMORIES OF PEOPLE 🧠💭
Inner Thoughts
- Short memories about someone
- Stored in that card’s Notes
- Uses a hidden tag {title: Name} so the script knows which card
- Stable version of the old auto-memory idea

Inner Opinions
- How people feel about each other
- Overall vibe of the chapter
- Favors, debts, secrets, social pressure
- The “living characters” social stuff that is NOT raw memory

Player Journal
- Your adventure in short lines
- Example: “You got engaged.”
- Example: “The dragon was defeated by you.”

────────────────────────────────
11. MAKING CARDS 🃏
Card Creator only creates cards.
It can guess Character / Location / Other.
You can also type:
/card Character Bob tall scarred hunter

Format cards (_Character_, _Location_, _Other_) show the field layout.
You may edit those formats.

────────────────────────────────
12. HOW THE STORY SOUNDS 🎭
Narration controls:
- Does the AI write your character or only NPCs?
- Present or past tense
- Pacing and “don’t use bad AI habits”

Story Splices = flavor only (Western, Noir, Sitcom, etc.).
They do not change the plot.
Speech: Ebonics (or another pattern) on a character card
makes that character talk that way.

Special devices:
- Confessional Cutaway = private “talk to the reader” then snap back
- Deadpool Break = in-scene fourth wall (player), with cooldown
- Bombastic Superhero = pose on big declarations
- Live Studio Audience = laughs/gasps as flavor

────────────────────────────────
13. BIG STORY MOVES 🌀
Sleeve Jump = new body, same mind (Altered Carbon style)
Shift = jump in time on purpose; return with changes
Isekai = one-way trip to another world; you are still you
(not “start already as a new OC”)

────────────────────────────────
14. WERE / MER 🐺🌊
Optional transformation rules:
daylight, every night, anger, feeding, permanent effects.
You configure what is true in your story.

────────────────────────────────
15. SAFETY & QoL 🛡️
Information Firewall = characters don’t know meta / out-of-scene stuff
Standard Rules = baseline “write clean, keep pronouns straight…”
Your own extra rules: cards named Aetherheart Rules, Aetherheart Rules 2…
Silent Mode = system messages don’t spam the story
/save and /load = move your state between adventures (coded block)
/reset memory vs /reset all
Game Over screen if HP hits 0

────────────────────────────────
16. HOW TO TALK TO IT
You usually don’t need commands.
Talk like a person:
“check the job board”
“equip the leather vest”
“rest until morning”
“take the bounty on Vex”

Commands are there when you want a clean dump:
/status /inventory /time /help /keywords

────────────────────────────────
17. FREE / LOW CONTEXT
Scripts themselves don’t eat your context budget the same way
story text does.
Aetherheart tries to inject only what’s needed.
Turn OFF what you don’t use.
That’s the point.

────────────────────────────────
18. IF SOMETHING FEELS BROKEN
1. Is the system ON in Configure?
2. Did you wait one turn after changing a toggle?
3. /status and /help
4. /jailbreak for troubleshooting (suspends story pressure)
5. /reset memory if only memories are weird

────────────────────────────────
You didn’t need to memorize this.
Turn on one system.
Play.
Turn on the next when you want it.

🐉🔮 That’s Aetherheart. 🔮🐉
Assemble the code today when you can.

Then GitHub + these two manuals in the repo (README-basic.md + MANUAL.md or similar).

Do you actually need Git?

Goal	Need Git?
Just store files online for yourself	No — you can drag files into GitHub in the browser
Share a link so others can download	No — browser upload is enough
Update often, track changes, look professional	Yes — Git (or GitHub Desktop) helps
Work with other people later	Yes

i dont need a script for context in ai dungeon right? just installed it all and dont see the startup card in the scenario grok...


Worked for 34s
You don’t need a separate “context script” for what we built.
Input + Output + Library is enough. (Some setups have a Context tab — we didn’t require it.)

Why you don’t see the Configure card

The card is only created when the Library script actually runs. That usually means:

Scripts are saved on the scenario (not only an adventure)
You start a new adventure from that scenario (or take a turn so Library/Output runs)
There is no error in Library that stops it before writeCard
Check in order

Scenario → Scripts
Library has the big code
Input / Output have the small wrappers that call onInput_Aetherheart / onOutput_Aetherheart
Save the scenario.
New adventure from that scenario (old adventures often won’t pick up Library changes cleanly).
Take one action (or Continue once).
Look in Story Cards for something like
Configure Aetherheart
or under a container starting with · Aetherheart / 🐉🔮 if you already renamed.
If it’s still missing

Library is probably erroring. Common causes with our stack:

addStoryCard(...) argument order doesn’t match current AI Dungeon
btoa / atob or something else throwing on boot
Function used before it’s defined because pieces were pasted out of order
Quick test:
At the very top of Library, temporarily add something that always runs, or simplify boot to only:

addStoryCard("Configure Aetherheart", "Script is running", "Aetherheart")
If that card appears, the script slot works and the bug is inside our longer boot.

function findCard(title) {
  try {
    if (typeof storyCards === "undefined" || !storyCards) return null;
    for (var i = 0; i < storyCards.length; i++) {
      if (storyCards[i] && storyCards[i].title === title) return storyCards[i];
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return null;
}

function writeCard(title, entry, category) {
  category = category || "Aetherheart";
  try {
    var card = findCard(title);
    if (card) {
      card.entry = entry;
      if (category && card.type !== undefined) card.type = category;
      return;
    }
    // try common AID signatures
    try {
      addStoryCard(title, entry, category);
      return;
    } catch (e1) {}
    try {
      addStoryCard({ title: title, entry: entry, type: category, keys: title });
      return;
    } catch (e2) {}
    try {
      addStoryCard(title, entry);
    } catch (e3) {}
  } catch (e) {}
}

function getMonthName(m) {
  var names = ["January","February","March","April","May","June","July","August","September","October","November","December"];
  return names[(m || 1) - 1] || "Unknown";
}

function formatHour(h) {
  var hr = Math.floor(h || 0);
  var ampm = hr >= 12 ? "PM" : "AM";
  var d = hr % 12 === 0 ? 12 : hr % 12;
  return d + ":00 " + ampm;
}

function parseOnOff(block, key) {
  if (!block) return null;
  var re = new RegExp(key + "\\s*:\\s*(ON|OFF)", "i");
  var m = block.match(re);
  if (!m) return null;
  return m[1].toUpperCase() === "ON";
}

function parseValue(block, key) {
  if (!block) return null;
  var re = new RegExp(key + "\\s*:\\s*([^\\n]+)", "i");
  var m = block.match(re);
  return m ? m[1].trim() : null;
}

function pushMsg(A, msg) {
  if (!msg) return;
  try {
    if (A.settings.silentMode) {
      A.messages = A.messages || [];
      A.messages.push(msg);
      if (A.messages.length > 20) A.messages = A.messages.slice(-15);
    } else {
      state.message = (state.message ? state.message + "\n" : "") + "[SYSTEM] " + msg;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

function buildStatusCard(A) {
  var lines = [];
  var t = A.time || {};
  if (A.settings.timeEnabled) {
    lines.push(getMonthName(t.month) + " " + t.day + ", Year " + t.year + " | " + formatHour(t.hour));
  }
  if (A.settings.vitals && A.vitals) {
    var v = A.vitals;
    lines.push("HP " + v.hp + "/" + v.maxHp + "  MP " + v.mp + "/" + v.maxMp + "  STA " + v.stamina + "/" + v.maxStamina);
  }
  if (A.settings.survival && A.survival) {
    var s = A.survival;
    lines.push("Hunger " + s.hunger + "%  Thirst " + s.thirst + "%  Tired " + s.tiredness + "%");
  }
  if (A.settings.fuel) lines.push((A.settings.fuelName || "Fuel") + ": " + (A.fuel || 100) + "%");
  if (A.settings.leveling) lines.push("Level " + A.level + "  XP " + A.xp + "/" + A.xpToNext);
  if (A.settings.currencyEnabled) lines.push("Balance " + (A.currencySymbol || "$") + (A.balance || 0));
  return "== STATUS ==\n" + (lines.length ? lines.join("\n") : "(no active meters)");
}

    "Rest / refuel work when those systems are ON\n" +
    "Data is kept when a system is turned OFF";
}

}

function readConfigureCard(A) {
  var card = findCard("Configure Aetherheart");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  var e = card.entry;
  var preset = parseValue(e, "Preset");
  if (preset) A.settings.preset = preset.split(/\s|\|/)[0].toLowerCase();
  var rating = parseValue(e, "Rating");
  if (rating) A.settings.rating = rating.split(/\s|\|/)[0];
  var map = [
    ["Script", "scriptEnabled"],
    ["Inventory", "inventoryEnabled"],
    ["Currency", "currencyEnabled"],
    ["Time", "timeEnabled"],
    ["Bills", "billsEnabled"],
    ["Currency context", "currencyContext"],
    ["Narration System", "narrationSystem"],
    ["Imperial units", "imperial"],
    ["RP System", "rpSystem"],
    ["Inner Thoughts", "innerThoughts"],
    ["Inner Opinions", "innerOpinions"],
    ["Card Creator", "cardCreator"],
    ["Scheduling", "scheduling"],
    ["Bounty Board", "bountyBoard"],
    ["Job Board", "jobBoard"],
    ["Quests", "quests"]
  ];
  for (var i = 0; i < map.length; i++) {
    var v = parseOnOff(e, map[i][0]);
    if (v !== null) A.settings[map[i][1]] = v;
  }
  var cn = parseValue(e, "Currency name");
  if (cn) A.currencyName = cn.split(/\n/)[0].trim();
  var cs = parseValue(e, "Currency symbol");
  if (cs) A.currencySymbol = cs.split(/\n/)[0].trim();
}

function readRPSystemCard(A) {
  var card = findCard("RP System");
  if (!card || !card.entry) return;
  var e = card.entry;
  var map = [
    ["Vitals","vitals"],["Survival","survival"],["Leveling","leveling"],
    ["Skills","skills"],["Abilities","abilities"],["Powers","powers"],
    ["Loot","loot"],["Ammo","ammo"],["d20","d20"],["Fuel","fuel"],
    ["Reputation","reputation"],["Jail","jail"],["Momentum","momentum"],
    ["Deus Ex","deusEx"],["Anti-Deus Ex","antiDeusEx"]
  ];
  for (var i = 0; i < map.length; i++) {
    var v = parseOnOff(e, map[i][0]);
    if (v !== null) A.settings[map[i][1]] = v;
  }
  var fn = parseValue(e, "Fuel Name");
  if (fn) A.settings.fuelName = fn.split(/\n/)[0].trim();
  var mm = parseValue(e, "Magic Mode");
  if (mm) {
    mm = mm.toLowerCase();
    if (mm.indexOf("wizard") === 0) A.settings.magicMode = "wizarding";
    else if (mm.indexOf("sorc") === 0) A.settings.magicMode = "sorcery";
  }
  if (!A.settings.leveling) {
    A.settings.skills = false;
    A.settings.abilities = false;
    A.settings.powers = false;
    A.settings.loot = false;
  }
}

function syncFromCards(A) {
  try { readConfigureCard(A); } catch (e) {}
  try { readRPSystemCard(A); } catch (e) {}
}

function parseTimePerTurn(str) {
  if (!str) return 5;
  var m = String(str).trim().match(/^(\d+)\s*(m|min|h|hr|hrs)?/i);
  if (!m) return 5;
  var n = parseInt(m[1], 10);
  var u = (m[2] || "m").toLowerCase();
  if (u.indexOf("h") === 0) return n * 60;
  return n;
}

function advanceTime(A, minutes) {
  if (!A.settings.timeEnabled) return;
  var t = A.time || { year: 632, month: 4, day: 12, hour: 8 };
  var total = Math.floor((t.hour || 0) * 60) + (minutes || 0);
  var dayAdd = Math.floor(total / (24 * 60));
  total = total % (24 * 60);
  t.hour = total / 60;
  t.day = (t.day || 1) + dayAdd;
  while (t.day > 30) { t.day -= 30; t.month++; }
  while (t.month > 12) { t.month -= 12; t.year++; }
  A.time = t;
}

function tickSurvival(A, minutes) {
  if (!A.settings.survival) return;
  var s = A.survival;
  var f = Math.max(1, Math.floor((minutes || 5) / 5));
  s.hunger = Math.min(100, (s.hunger || 0) + f);
  s.thirst = Math.min(100, (s.thirst || 0) + f + 1);
  s.tiredness = Math.min(100, (s.tiredness || 0) + Math.floor(f / 2));
}

function handleD20(text, A) {
  if (!A.settings.d20) return null;
  var m = text.match(/roll for (attack|saving|ability check(?:\s*\(([^)]+)\))?|initiative)/i);
  if (!m) return null;
  var roll = Math.floor(Math.random() * 20) + 1;
  return "d20 " + m[1] + " → " + roll;
}

function processTurn(text, A) {
  var mins = parseTimePerTurn(A.time && A.time.timePerTurn);
  advanceTime(A, mins);
  tickSurvival(A, mins);

    A.vitals.stamina = A.vitals.maxStamina;

    if (A.settings.survival) A.survival.tiredness = Math.max(0, A.survival.tiredness - 40);
    advanceTime(A, 360);
    pushMsg(A, "Rested");
  }

  }

    pushMsg(A, "Bounty Board checked");
  }
  if (A.settings.jobBoard && /check (the )?job board|look for work/.test(t)) {
    pushMsg(A, "Job Board checked");
  }
  if (A.settings.currencyEnabled && /check (my )?(balance|wallet|money)/.
